Glossary candidates: mahskill

The names this department's GRs give most, found by code: the officers who sign, the short text of table cells, the entries of the copy lists. The number of GRs opens them in Studio, one after another ([ and ]). A name that is not a name, or one whose English is wrong, is marked in review/glossary/top.tsv: x in ok to leave it out (it is then never translated), or the right English in correct en; the next make takes the marks in. The files on this page are made again by every make: a mark made here would be lost.
